gp_colors    = "🎨 Цветовая схема MOEX"
c_bull       = input.color(#22c55e, "Цвет Бычьих Зон MOEX / LONG", group=gp_colors)
c_bear       = input.color(#ef4444, "Цвет Медвежьих Зон MOEX / SHORT", group=gp_colors)
c_gold       = input.color(#eab308, "Цвет Сигналов MOEX GOLD", group=gp_colors)
c_fuchsia    = input.color(#d946ef, "Цвет Пурпурных Сигналов", group=gp_colors)
c_neutral    = input.color(#94a3b8, "Нейтральный Цвет", group=gp_colors)

// ==========================================================
// 🛠️ ВСПОМОГАТЕЛЬНЫЕ ФУНКЦИИ MOEX
// ==========================================================
get_text_size(opt) =>
    opt == "Микро" ? size.tiny : opt == "Маленький" ? size.small : opt == "Обычный" ? size.normal : opt == "Крупный" ? size.large : size.small

get_table_pos(pos) =>
    pos == "Верхний левый" ? position.top_left : pos == "Верхний правый" ? position.top_right : pos == "Нижний левый" ? position.bottom_left : pos == "Нижний правый" ? position.bottom_right : position.bottom_right

// ATR и Базовые индикаторы
atr = ta.atr(14)
sma50 = ta.sma(close, 50)
ema_trend = ta.ema(close, 200)

cross_bull = ta.crossover(close, ema_trend)
cross_bear = ta.crossunder(close, ema_trend)

v_buy  = volume * (close >= open ? (high - low > 0 ? (close - low) / (high - low) : 0.5) : (high - low > 0 ? (high - open) / (high - low) : 0.3))
v_sell = volume - v_buy
v_delta = v_buy - v_sell
v_delta_ma  = ta.sma(v_delta, 20)
v_delta_std = ta.stdev(v_delta, 20)

vol_sma = ta.sma(volume, 20)
is_extreme_delta = math.abs(v_delta) > vol_sma * 1.8
is_extreme_buy  = is_extreme_delta and v_delta > 0
is_extreme_sell = is_extreme_delta and v_delta < 0

// Индикаторы Моментума (RSI + MACD)
rsi_val = ta.rsi(close, 14)
rsi_bull_ok = rsi_val >= 50 and rsi_val <= 70
rsi_bear_ok = rsi_val <= 50 and rsi_val >= 30

[macd_line, macd_sig, _] = ta.macd(close, 12, 26, 9)
macd_bull_ok = macd_line > macd_sig
macd_bear_ok = macd_line < macd_sig

osc_buy_ok  = rsi_bull_ok or macd_bull_ok
osc_sell_ok = rsi_bear_ok or macd_bear_ok

// Межрыночный макро-анализ MOEX (IMOEX, DXY, BR1!)
imoex_close = use_moex ? request.security("MOEX:IMOEX", imoex_tf, close, ignore_invalid_symbol=true) : na
imoex_ema   = use_moex ? request.security("MOEX:IMOEX", imoex_tf, ta.ema(close, 20), ignore_invalid_symbol=true) : na
imoex_bull  = use_moex and not na(imoex_close) and not na(imoex_ema) ? imoex_close > imoex_ema : false
imoex_bear  = use_moex and not na(imoex_close) and not na(imoex_ema) ? imoex_close < imoex_ema : false

dxy_close = use_macro ? request.security("CAPITALCOM:DXY", timeframe.period, close, ignore_invalid_symbol=true) : na
dxy_ema   = use_macro ? request.security("CAPITALCOM:DXY", timeframe.period, ta.ema(close, 20), ignore_invalid_symbol=true) : na
dxy_bull  = use_macro and not na(dxy_close) and not na(dxy_ema) ? dxy_close > dxy_ema : false
dxy_bear  = use_macro and not na(dxy_close) and not na(dxy_ema) ? dxy_close < dxy_ema : false

oil_close = use_macro ? request.security("MOEX:BR1!", timeframe.period, close, ignore_invalid_symbol=true) : na
oil_ema   = use_macro ? request.security("MOEX:BR1!", timeframe.period, ta.ema(close, 20), ignore_invalid_symbol=true) : na
oil_bull  = use_macro and not na(oil_close) and not na(oil_ema) ? oil_close > oil_ema : false

// Pivots SMC MOEX
ph_lt = ta.pivothigh(high, ob_pivot_len, ob_pivot_len)
pl_lt = ta.pivotlow(low, ob_pivot_len, ob_pivot_len)

var float last_ph = na
var float last_pl = na

if not na(ph_lt)
    last_ph := ph_lt
if not na(pl_lt)
    last_pl := pl_lt

// Зоны поддержки и сопротивления MOEX
var box[] sup_boxes = array.new_box(0)
var box[] res_boxes = array.new_box(0)
var label[] sup_labels = array.new_label(0)
var label[] res_labels = array.new_label(0)

draw_sr_box(float p_level, bool is_support, string label_text, int offset) =>
    bg_color = is_support ? color.new(c_bull, 85) : color.new(c_bear, 85)
    border_color = is_support ? color.new(c_bull, 60) : color.new(c_bear, 60)
    textcolor = is_support ? c_bull : c_bear
    
    bool merged = false
    if merge_sr
        if is_support and array.size(sup_boxes) > 0
            for i = 0 to array.size(sup_boxes) - 1
                bx = array.get(sup_boxes, i)
                lbl = array.get(sup_labels, i)
                b_top = box.get_top(bx)
                b_bottom = box.get_bottom(bx)
                float box_center = (b_top + b_bottom) / 2.0
                if math.abs(p_level - box_center) < atr * 0.65
                    new_top = math.max(b_top, p_level + atr * 0.08)
                    new_bottom = math.min(b_bottom, p_level - atr * 0.08)
                    box.set_top(bx, new_top)
                    box.set_bottom(bx, new_bottom)
                    label.set_y(lbl, (new_top + new_bottom) / 2.0)
                    label.set_text(lbl, label_text)
                    merged := true
                    break
        else if not is_support and array.size(res_boxes) > 0
            for i = 0 to array.size(res_boxes) - 1
                bx = array.get(res_boxes, i)
                lbl = array.get(res_labels, i)
                b_top = box.get_top(bx)
                b_bottom = box.get_bottom(bx)
                float box_center = (b_top + b_bottom) / 2.0
                if math.abs(p_level - box_center) < atr * 0.65
                    new_top = math.max(b_top, p_level + atr * 0.08)
                    new_bottom = math.min(b_bottom, p_level - atr * 0.08)
                    box.set_top(bx, new_top)
                    box.set_bottom(bx, new_bottom)
                    label.set_y(lbl, (new_top + new_bottom) / 2.0)
                    label.set_text(lbl, label_text)
                    merged := true
                    break

    if not merged
        b = box.new(left=bar_index - offset, top=p_level + atr * 0.08, right=bar_index + label_offset, bottom=p_level - atr * 0.08, bgcolor=bg_color, border_color=border_color, border_style=line.style_dashed)
        l = label.new(bar_index + label_offset, p_level, text=label_text, style=label.style_label_left, color=color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor=textcolor, size=get_text_size(text_sz_opt))
        if is_support
            array.push(sup_boxes, b)
            array.push(sup_labels, l)
            if array.size(sup_boxes) > max_sr_boxes
                box.delete(array.shift(sup_boxes))
                label.delete(array.shift(sup_labels))
        else
            array.push(res_boxes, b)
            array.push(res_labels, l)
            if array.size(res_boxes) > max_sr_boxes
                box.delete(array.shift(res_boxes))
                label.delete(array.shift(res_labels))

add_sr_zone(bool is_supp, float p_lvl, int off, string txt) =>
    draw_sr_box(p_lvl, is_supp, txt, off)

if show_sr_zones
    if not na(ph_lt)
        p_vol = volume[ob_pivot_len * 3]
        add_sr_zone(false, ph_lt, ob_pivot_len * 3, "Бычья Liquidity Зона MOEX (" + str.tostring(ph_lt, "#.##") + ") [" + str.tostring(p_vol / 1000.0, "#.#") + "K]")
    if not na(pl_lt)
        p_vol = volume[ob_pivot_len * 3]
        add_sr_zone(true, pl_lt, ob_pivot_len * 3, "Медвежья Liquidity Зона MOEX (" + str.tostring(pl_lt, "#.##") + ") [" + str.tostring(p_vol / 1000.0, "#.#") + "K]")

if barstate.islast and show_sr_zones
    if array.size(res_boxes) > 0
        for i = 0 to array.size(res_boxes) - 1
            box.set_right(array.get(res_boxes, i), bar_index + label_offset)
            label.set_x(array.get(res_labels, i), bar_index + label_offset)
    if array.size(sup_boxes) > 0
        for i = 0 to array.size(sup_boxes) - 1
            box.set_right(array.get(sup_boxes, i), bar_index + label_offset)
            label.set_x(array.get(sup_labels, i), bar_index + label_offset)

// --- БЛОКИ ОРДЕРОВ MOEX (+OB / -OB) ---
var box[] bull_ob_boxes = array.new_box(0)
var box[] bear_ob_boxes = array.new_box(0)

if show_ob and bar_index > 1
    if close > high[1] and close[1] < open[1]
        b = box.new(left=bar_index-1, top=high[1], right=bar_index + label_offset, bottom=low[1], bgcolor=color.new(c_gold, 88), border_color=color.new(c_gold, 50), text="+OB MOEX (Бычий)", text_color=c_gold, text_size=size.tiny, text_halign="right", text_valign="bottom")
        array.push(bull_ob_boxes, b)
    if close < low[1] and close[1] > open[1]
        b = box.new(left=bar_index-1, top=high[1], right=bar_index + label_offset, bottom=low[1], bgcolor=color.new(c_fuchsia, 88), border_color=color.new(c_fuchsia, 50), text="-OB MOEX (Медвежий)", text_color=c_fuchsia, text_size=size.tiny, text_halign="right", text_valign="bottom")
        array.push(bear_ob_boxes, b)

    if clean_ob and array.size(bull_ob_boxes) > 0
        for i = array.size(bull_ob_boxes) - 1 to 0
            bx = array.get(bull_ob_boxes, i)
            if low < box.get_bottom(bx)
                box.delete(bx)
                array.remove(bull_ob_boxes, i)

    if clean_ob and array.size(bear_ob_boxes) > 0
        for i = array.size(bear_ob_boxes) - 1 to 0
            bx = array.get(bear_ob_boxes, i)
            if high > box.get_top(bx)
                box.delete(bx)
                array.remove(bear_ob_boxes, i)

    if array.size(bull_ob_boxes) > 0
        for i = 0 to array.size(bull_ob_boxes) - 1
            box.set_right(array.get(bull_ob_boxes, i), bar_index + label_offset)
    if array.size(bear_ob_boxes) > 0
        for i = 0 to array.size(bear_ob_boxes) - 1
            box.set_right(array.get(bear_ob_boxes, i), bar_index + label_offset)

    if array.size(bull_ob_boxes) > ob_limit
        box.delete(array.shift(bull_ob_boxes))
    if array.size(bear_ob_boxes) > ob_limit
        box.delete(array.shift(bear_ob_boxes))

// --- FAIR VALUE GAPS MOEX (FVG) ---
var box[] bull_fvg_boxes = array.new_box(0)
var box[] bear_fvg_boxes = array.new_box(0)

if show_fvg and bar_index > 2
    if low > high[2] and close[1] > open[1]
        array.push(bull_fvg_boxes, box.new(left=bar_index-2, top=low, right=bar_index + label_offset, bottom=high[2], bgcolor=color.new(c_bull, 92), border_color=color.new(c_bull, 70), border_style=line.style_dashed, text="+FVG MOEX", text_color=c_bull, text_size=size.tiny, text_halign="right", text_valign="bottom"))
    if high < low[2] and close[1] < open[1]
        array.push(bear_fvg_boxes, box.new(left=bar_index-2, top=low[2], right=bar_index + label_offset, bottom=high, bgcolor=color.new(c_bear, 92), border_color=color.new(c_bear, 70), border_style=line.style_dashed, text="-FVG MOEX", text_color=c_bear, text_size=size.tiny, text_halign="right", text_valign="bottom"))

    if clean_ob and array.size(bull_fvg_boxes) > 0
        for i = array.size(bull_fvg_boxes) - 1 to 0
            bx = array.get(bull_fvg_boxes, i)
            if low < box.get_bottom(bx)
                box.delete(bx)
                array.remove(bull_fvg_boxes, i)

    if clean_ob and array.size(bear_fvg_boxes) > 0
        for i = array.size(bear_fvg_boxes) - 1 to 0
            bx = array.get(bear_fvg_boxes, i)
            if high > box.get_top(bx)
                box.delete(bx)
                array.remove(bear_fvg_boxes, i)

    if array.size(bull_fvg_boxes) > 0
        for i = 0 to array.size(bull_fvg_boxes) - 1
            box.set_right(array.get(bull_fvg_boxes, i), bar_index + label_offset)
    if array.size(bear_fvg_boxes) > 0
        for i = 0 to array.size(bear_fvg_boxes) - 1
            box.set_right(array.get(bear_fvg_boxes, i), bar_index + label_offset)

    if array.size(bull_fvg_boxes) > ob_limit
        box.delete(array.shift(bull_fvg_boxes))
    if array.size(bear_fvg_boxes) > ob_limit
        box.delete(array.shift(bear_fvg_boxes))

// --- SWEEP И LIQUIDITY SWEEPS MOEX ---
is_high_sweep = not na(last_ph) and high > last_ph and close <= last_ph
is_low_sweep  = not na(last_pl) and low < last_pl and close >= last_pl

plotshape(show_sweeps and is_high_sweep, title="MOEX High Liquidity Sweep", style=shape.triangledown, location=location.abovebar, color=color.orange, size=size.small, text="⚡ SWEEP MOEX", textcolor=color.orange)
plotshape(show_sweeps and is_low_sweep, title="MOEX Low Liquidity Sweep", style=shape.triangleup, location=location.belowbar, color=color.orange, size=size.small, text="⚡ SWEEP MOEX", textcolor=color.orange)

// --- ЗОНА OPTIMAL TRADE ENTRY MOEX (OTE 0.618 - 0.786) ---
lb_ote_period = 40
ote_hi        = ta.highest(high, lb_ote_period)
ote_lo        = ta.lowest(low, lb_ote_period)
ote_rng       = math.max(ote_hi - ote_lo, atr * 2.0)

is_ote_bull   = close >= sma50
ote_top_val   = is_ote_bull ? (ote_hi - ote_rng * 0.618) : (ote_lo + ote_rng * 0.786)
ote_bot_val   = is_ote_bull ? (ote_hi - ote_rng * 0.786) : (ote_lo + ote_rng * 0.618)

ote_zone_hi   = math.max(ote_top_val, ote_bot_val)
ote_zone_lo   = math.min(ote_top_val, ote_bot_val)

in_ote_zone   = close >= ote_zone_lo and close <= ote_zone_hi

var box   ote_box = na
var line  ote_l1  = na
var line  ote_l2  = na
var label ote_lbl = na

if barstate.islast and show_ote
    box.delete(ote_box)
    line.delete(ote_l1)
    line.delete(ote_l2)
    label.delete(ote_lbl)
    
    ote_clr = is_ote_bull ? c_bull : c_bear
    ote_txt = is_ote_bull ? "🎯 OTE MOEX 61.8-78.6% [Покупка]" : "🎯 OTE MOEX 61.8-78.6% [Продажа]"
    
    ote_box := box.new(left=bar_index - 35, top=ote_zone_hi, right=bar_index + label_offset + 8, bottom=ote_zone_lo, bgcolor=color.new(ote_clr, 93), border_color=color.new(ote_clr, 60), border_style=line.style_dashed)
    ote_l1  := line.new(bar_index - 35, ote_zone_hi, bar_index + label_offset + 8, ote_zone_hi, color=color.new(ote_clr, 35), width=1, style=line.style_dashed)
    ote_l2  := line.new(bar_index - 35, ote_zone_lo, bar_index + label_offset + 8, ote_zone_lo, color=color.new(ote_clr, 35), width=1, style=line.style_dashed)
    ote_lbl := label.new(bar_index + label_offset + 8, ote_zone_hi, ote_txt + (in_ote_zone ? " 🔥 [ЦЕНА В ЗОНЕ]" : ""), color=color.new(color.black, 100), textcolor=ote_clr, style=label.style_label_left, size=get_text_size(text_sz_opt))

// ==========================================================
// 🎯 МУЛЬТИ-ФАКТОРНЫЙ ДИНАМИЧЕСКИЙ РАСЧЕТ ВЕРОЯТНОСТИ (WINRATE %) MOEX
// ==========================================================
calc_moex_winrate(is_buy_dir) =>
    float score = 55.0
    
    // 1. Межрыночный контекст (IMOEX, DXY, BR1!) (+15%)
    if use_macro
        if is_buy_dir
            if dxy_bear
                score := score + 8.0
            if oil_bull
                score := score + 7.0
        else
            if dxy_bull
                score := score + 8.0
            if not oil_bull
                score := score + 7.0

    if use_moex
        if is_buy_dir and imoex_bull
            score := score + 10.0
        else if not is_buy_dir and imoex_bear
            score := score + 10.0

    // 2. Сила аномалии объема Дельты MOEX (+12%)
    float delta_z = v_delta_std > 0 ? (math.abs(v_delta - v_delta_ma) / v_delta_std) : 1.0
    score := score + math.min(12.0, delta_z * 4.0)

    // 3. Подтверждение SMC MOEX (+10%)
    bool has_smc = is_buy_dir ? (array.size(bull_ob_boxes) > 0 or array.size(bull_fvg_boxes) > 0) : (array.size(bear_ob_boxes) > 0 or array.size(bear_fvg_boxes) > 0)
    if has_smc
        score := score + 10.0

    // 4. Конфлюэнция Моментума (RSI + MACD) (+10%)
    if is_buy_dir and osc_buy_ok
        score := score + 10.0
    else if not is_buy_dir and osc_sell_ok
        score := score + 10.0

    // 5. Конфлюэнция OTE (+8%)
    if in_ote_zone
        score := score + 8.0

    math.min(95.0, math.max(45.0, score))

// ==========================================================
// 4. ВОЛНОВОЙ ПРОГНОЗ ЦЕНЫ И ВЕКТОРНЫЕ ЛИНИИ (FORECAST LEGS) MOEX
// ==========================================================
var line[] forecast_lines = array.new_line(0)
var label[] forecast_labels = array.new_label(0)

var int active_dir       = 0
var int active_bar       = na
var float active_y0      = na
var float active_entry   = na
var float active_sl      = na
var float active_bos     = na
var float active_tp      = na
var float active_alt     = na
var float active_winrate = na

clean_forecast() =>
    if array.size(forecast_lines) > 0
        for i = 0 to array.size(forecast_lines) - 1
            line.delete(array.get(forecast_lines, i))
        array.clear(forecast_lines)
    if array.size(forecast_labels) > 0
        for i = 0 to array.size(forecast_labels) - 1
            label.delete(array.get(forecast_labels, i))
        array.clear(forecast_labels)

has_bull_ob = array.size(bull_ob_boxes) > 0
has_bear_ob = array.size(bear_ob_boxes) > 0

buy_signal  = is_extreme_buy or (has_bull_ob and cross_bull)
sell_signal = is_extreme_sell or (has_bear_ob and cross_bear)

if buy_signal
    // 1. Учет Блоков Ордеров (OB) и FVG MOEX
    float ob_entry_bull = na
    float ob_sl_bull    = na
    if has_bull_ob
        bx_last = array.get(bull_ob_boxes, array.size(bull_ob_boxes) - 1)
        ob_entry_bull := box.get_top(bx_last)
        ob_sl_bull    := box.get_bottom(bx_last)
        
    float fvg_entry_bull = na
    if array.size(bull_fvg_boxes) > 0
        fb_last = array.get(bull_fvg_boxes, array.size(bull_fvg_boxes) - 1)
        fvg_entry_bull := (box.get_top(fb_last) + box.get_bottom(fb_last)) / 2.0
        
    // 2. Вход с учетом OTE, OB, FVG
    float entry_p = in_ote_zone ? (ote_zone_hi + ote_zone_lo) / 2.0 : not na(ob_entry_bull) ? ob_entry_bull : not na(fvg_entry_bull) ? fvg_entry_bull : math.min(low[1], low[2])
    
    // 3. Защитный Стоп-Лосс (SL) за нижнюю границу OB/Liquidity S/R с буфером ATR
    float ob_floor = math.min(low, math.min(low[1], low[2]))
    if not na(ob_sl_bull)
        ob_floor := math.min(ob_floor, ob_sl_bull)
    if is_low_sweep and not na(last_pl)
        ob_floor := math.min(ob_floor, last_pl)
    float sl_p = ob_floor - atr * sl_atr_mult
    
    // 4. Уровень BOS и Тейк-Профиты с учетом Конфлюэнции RSI/MACD
    float bos_ref = math.max(high, not na(last_ph) ? last_ph : high * 1.01)
    float mom_mult = (rsi_bull_ok and macd_bull_ok) ? 1.15 : (rsi_bull_ok or macd_bull_ok) ? 1.05 : 0.95
    float wave_span = math.max(bos_ref - entry_p, atr * 1.5) * mom_mult
    
    float bos_p = entry_p + wave_span * 0.75
    float tp_p  = entry_p + wave_span * fibo_tp_ext
    float alt_p = sl_p - wave_span * 0.8
    
    active_dir     := 1
    active_bar     := bar_index
    active_y0      := close
    active_entry   := entry_p
    active_sl      := sl_p
    active_bos     := bos_p
    active_tp      := tp_p
    active_alt     := alt_p
    active_winrate := calc_moex_winrate(true)

if sell_signal
    // 1. Учет Медвежьих Блоков Ордеров (OB) и FVG MOEX
    float ob_entry_bear = na
    float ob_sl_bear    = na
    if has_bear_ob
        bx_last = array.get(bear_ob_boxes, array.size(bear_ob_boxes) - 1)
        ob_entry_bear := box.get_bottom(bx_last)
        ob_sl_bear    := box.get_top(bx_last)
        
    float fvg_entry_bear = na
    if array.size(bear_fvg_boxes) > 0
        fb_last = array.get(bear_fvg_boxes, array.size(bear_fvg_boxes) - 1)
        fvg_entry_bear := (box.get_top(fb_last) + box.get_bottom(fb_last)) / 2.0
        
    // 2. Вход с учетом OTE, OB, FVG
    float entry_p = in_ote_zone ? (ote_zone_hi + ote_zone_lo) / 2.0 : not na(ob_entry_bear) ? ob_entry_bear : not na(fvg_entry_bear) ? fvg_entry_bear : math.max(high[1], high[2])
    
    // 3. Защитный Стоп-Лосс (SL) за верхнюю границу OB/Liquidity S/R с буфером ATR
    float ob_roof = math.max(high, math.max(high[1], high[2]))
    if not na(ob_sl_bear)
        ob_roof := math.max(ob_roof, ob_sl_bear)
    if is_high_sweep and not na(last_ph)
        ob_roof := math.max(ob_roof, last_ph)
    float sl_p = ob_roof + atr * sl_atr_mult
    
    // 4. Уровень BOS и Тейк-Профиты с учетом Конфлюэнции RSI/MACD
    float bos_ref = math.min(low, not na(last_pl) ? last_pl : low * 0.99)
    float mom_mult = (rsi_bear_ok and macd_bear_ok) ? 1.15 : (rsi_bear_ok or macd_bear_ok) ? 1.05 : 0.95
    float wave_span = math.max(entry_p - bos_ref, atr * 1.5) * mom_mult
    
    float bos_p = entry_p - wave_span * 0.75
    float tp_p  = entry_p - wave_span * fibo_tp_ext
    float alt_p = sl_p + wave_span * 0.8
    
    active_dir     := -1
    active_bar     := bar_index
    active_y0      := close
    active_entry   := entry_p
    active_sl      := sl_p
    active_bos     := bos_p
    active_tp      := tp_p
    active_alt     := alt_p
    active_winrate := calc_moex_winrate(false)

// Отрисовка динамического волнового прогноза от свечи сигнала MOEX
if barstate.islast and show_forecast
    clean_forecast()
    
    curr_dir = active_dir != 0 ? active_dir : ((use_moex ? imoex_bull : true) and close >= sma50 ? 1 : -1)
    
    x0 = not na(active_bar) ? active_bar : bar_index
    y0 = not na(active_y0) ? active_y0 : close
    
    x1 = math.max(bar_index + 4, x0 + 8)
    x2 = math.max(bar_index + 12, x0 + 18)
    x3 = math.max(bar_index + 20, x0 + 30)
    end_x = math.max(x3 + 8, bar_index + label_offset + 15)
    
    if curr_dir == 1
        entry_p = not na(active_entry) ? active_entry : math.min(low[1], low[2])
        sl_p    = not na(active_sl) ? active_sl : entry_p - atr * sl_atr_mult
        bos_p   = not na(active_bos) ? active_bos : entry_p + atr * 1.5
        tp_p    = not na(active_tp) ? active_tp : entry_p + atr * 1.5 * fibo_tp_ext
        alt_y2  = not na(active_alt) ? active_alt : sl_p - atr * 1.2
        wr_val  = active_winrate
        
        float risk   = math.abs(entry_p - sl_p)
        float reward = math.abs(tp_p - entry_p)
        float rr_val = risk > 0 ? reward / risk : 3.0
        
        y1 = entry_p
        y2 = bos_p
        y3 = tp_p
        
        l01 = line.new(x1=x0, y1=y0, x2=x1, y2=y1, xloc=xloc.bar_index, color=c_gold, width=2, style=line.style_dashed)
        l12 = line.new(x1=x1, y1=y1, x2=x2, y2=y2, xloc=xloc.bar_index, color=#38bdf8, width=2, style=line.style_solid)
        l23 = line.new(x1=x2, y1=y2, x2=x3, y2=y3, xloc=xloc.bar_index, color=c_bull, width=3, style=line.style_solid)
        
        array.push(forecast_lines, l01)
        array.push(forecast_lines, l12)
        array.push(forecast_lines, l23)
        
        lbl0 = label.new(x0, y0, "Point 0 [Старт]", color=color.new(#3b82f6, 10), textcolor=color.white, style=label.style_label_down, size=get_text_size(text_sz_opt))
        lbl1 = label.new(x1, y1, "🎯 ВХОД (Buy Limit): " + str.tostring(entry_p, "#.##"), color=color.new(c_gold, 10), textcolor=color.black, style=label.style_label_up, size=get_text_size(text_sz_opt))
        lbl2 = label.new(x2, y2, "Leg 2: BOS MOEX ↑ (" + str.tostring(bos_p, "#.##") + ")", color=color.new(#38bdf8, 10), textcolor=color.white, style=label.style_label_down, size=get_text_size(text_sz_opt))
        lbl3 = label.new(x3, y3, "🎯 МАКСИМАЛЬНЫЙ ТЕЙК (TP 1.618): " + str.tostring(tp_p, "#.##") + " [" + str.tostring(wr_val, "#.#") + "%]", color=color.new(c_bull, 0), textcolor=color.white, style=label.style_label_down, size=size.normal)
        
        array.push(forecast_labels, lbl0)
        array.push(forecast_labels, lbl1)
        array.push(forecast_labels, lbl2)
        array.push(forecast_labels, lbl3)
        
        if show_alt_wave
            alt_x1 = math.max(bar_index + 4, x0 + 6)
            alt_y1 = sl_p
            alt_x2 = math.max(bar_index + 12, x0 + 16)
            
            alt_l1 = line.new(x1=x0, y1=y0, x2=alt_x1, y2=alt_y1, xloc=xloc.bar_index, color=color.new(c_neutral, 40), width=2, style=line.style_dotted)
            alt_l2 = line.new(x1=alt_x1, y1=alt_y1, x2=alt_x2, y2=alt_y2, xloc=xloc.bar_index, color=color.new(c_fuchsia, 40), width=2, style=line.style_dotted)
            
            array.push(forecast_lines, alt_l1)
            array.push(forecast_lines, alt_l2)
            
            lbl_alt = label.new(alt_x2, alt_y2, "⚠️ АЛЬТЕРНАТИВА: Слом SL (" + str.tostring(sl_p, "#.##") + ") ➔ Цель Дампа " + str.tostring(alt_y2, "#.##"), color=color.new(c_fuchsia, 20), textcolor=color.white, style=label.style_label_up, size=get_text_size(text_sz_opt))
            array.push(forecast_labels, lbl_alt)
            
        if show_trade_lvl
            l_entry = line.new(x1=x0, y1=entry_p, x2=end_x, y2=entry_p, xloc=xloc.bar_index, color=c_gold, width=2, style=line.style_dashed)
            l_sl    = line.new(x1=x0, y1=sl_p, x2=end_x, y2=sl_p, xloc=xloc.bar_index, color=c_bear, width=2, style=line.style_solid)
            l_tp    = line.new(x1=x3, y1=tp_p, x2=end_x, y2=tp_p, xloc=xloc.bar_index, color=c_bull, width=2, style=line.style_solid)
            
            lbl_entry_txt = label.new(end_x, entry_p, "📌 ВХОД: " + str.tostring(entry_p, "#.##"), color=color.new(c_gold, 20), textcolor=color.black, style=label.style_label_left, size=get_text_size(text_sz_opt))
            lbl_sl_txt    = label.new(end_x, sl_p, "🛑 СТОП-ЛОСС (SL): " + str.tostring(sl_p, "#.##") + " [R:R 1:" + str.tostring(rr_val, "#.#") + "]", color=color.new(c_bear, 20), textcolor=color.white, style=label.style_label_left, size=get_text_size(text_sz_opt))
            lbl_tp_txt    = label.new(end_x, tp_p, "🎯 ТЕЙК (TP): " + str.tostring(tp_p, "#.##"), color=color.new(c_bull, 20), textcolor=color.white, style=label.style_label_left, size=get_text_size(text_sz_opt))
            
            array.push(forecast_lines, l_entry)
            array.push(forecast_lines, l_sl)
            array.push(forecast_lines, l_tp)
            array.push(forecast_labels, lbl_entry_txt)
            array.push(forecast_labels, lbl_sl_txt)
            array.push(forecast_labels, lbl_tp_txt)

    else
        entry_p = not na(active_entry) ? active_entry : math.max(high[1], high[2])
        sl_p    = not na(active_sl) ? active_sl : entry_p + atr * sl_atr_mult
        bos_p   = not na(active_bos) ? active_bos : entry_p - atr * 1.5
        tp_p    = not na(active_tp) ? active_tp : entry_p - atr * 1.5 * fibo_tp_ext
        alt_y2  = not na(active_alt) ? active_alt : sl_p + atr * 1.2
        wr_val  = active_winrate
        
        float risk   = math.abs(sl_p - entry_p)
        float reward = math.abs(entry_p - tp_p)
        float rr_val = risk > 0 ? reward / risk : 3.0
        
        y1 = entry_p
        y2 = bos_p
        y3 = tp_p
        
        l01 = line.new(x1=x0, y1=y0, x2=x1, y2=y1, xloc=xloc.bar_index, color=c_fuchsia, width=2, style=line.style_dashed)
        l12 = line.new(x1=x1, y1=y1, x2=x2, y2=y2, xloc=xloc.bar_index, color=#f43f5e, width=2, style=line.style_solid)
        l23 = line.new(x1=x2, y1=y2, x2=x3, y2=y3, xloc=xloc.bar_index, color=c_bear, width=3, style=line.style_solid)
        
        array.push(forecast_lines, l01)
        array.push(forecast_lines, l12)
        array.push(forecast_lines, l23)
        
        lbl0 = label.new(x0, y0, "Point 0 [Старт]", color=color.new(#c026d3, 10), textcolor=color.white, style=label.style_label_up, size=get_text_size(text_sz_opt))
        lbl1 = label.new(x1, y1, "🎯 ВХОД (Sell Limit): " + str.tostring(entry_p, "#.##"), color=color.new(c_fuchsia, 10), textcolor=color.white, style=label.style_label_down, size=get_text_size(text_sz_opt))
        lbl2 = label.new(x2, y2, "Leg 2: BOS MOEX ↓ (" + str.tostring(bos_p, "#.##") + ")", color=color.new(#f43f5e, 10), textcolor=color.white, style=label.style_label_up, size=get_text_size(text_sz_opt))
        lbl3 = label.new(x3, y3, "🎯 МАКСИМАЛЬНЫЙ ТЕЙК (TP 1.618): " + str.tostring(tp_p, "#.##") + " [" + str.tostring(wr_val, "#.#") + "%]", color=color.new(c_bear, 0), textcolor=color.white, style=label.style_label_up, size=size.normal)
        
        array.push(forecast_labels, lbl0)
        array.push(forecast_labels, lbl1)
        array.push(forecast_labels, lbl2)
        array.push(forecast_labels, lbl3)
        
        if show_alt_wave
            alt_x1 = math.max(bar_index + 4, x0 + 6)
            alt_y1 = sl_p
            alt_x2 = math.max(bar_index + 12, x0 + 16)
            
            alt_l1 = line.new(x1=x0, y1=y0, x2=alt_x1, y2=alt_y1, xloc=xloc.bar_index, color=color.new(c_neutral, 40), width=2, style=line.style_dotted)
            alt_l2 = line.new(x1=alt_x1, y1=alt_y1, x2=alt_x2, y2=alt_y2, xloc=xloc.bar_index, color=color.new(c_gold, 40), width=2, style=line.style_dotted)
            
            array.push(forecast_lines, alt_l1)
            array.push(forecast_lines, alt_l2)
            
            lbl_alt = label.new(alt_x2, alt_y2, "⚠️ АЛЬТЕРНАТИВА: Слом SL (" + str.tostring(sl_p, "#.##") + ") ➔ Цель Пампа " + str.tostring(alt_y2, "#.##"), color=color.new(c_gold, 20), textcolor=color.black, style=label.style_label_down, size=get_text_size(text_sz_opt))
            array.push(forecast_labels, lbl_alt)
            
        if show_trade_lvl
            l_entry = line.new(x1=x0, y1=entry_p, x2=end_x, y2=entry_p, xloc=xloc.bar_index, color=c_fuchsia, width=2, style=line.style_dashed)
            l_sl    = line.new(x1=x0, y1=sl_p, x2=end_x, y2=sl_p, xloc=xloc.bar_index, color=c_bull, width=2, style=line.style_solid)
            l_tp    = line.new(x1=x3, y1=tp_p, x2=end_x, y2=tp_p, xloc=xloc.bar_index, color=c_bear, width=2, style=line.style_solid)
            
            lbl_entry_txt = label.new(end_x, entry_p, "📌 ВХОД: " + str.tostring(entry_p, "#.##"), color=color.new(c_fuchsia, 20), textcolor=color.white, style=label.style_label_left, size=get_text_size(text_sz_opt))
            lbl_sl_txt    = label.new(end_x, sl_p, "🛑 СТОП-ЛОСС (SL): " + str.tostring(sl_p, "#.##") + " [R:R 1:" + str.tostring(rr_val, "#.#") + "]", color=color.new(c_bull, 20), textcolor=color.black, style=label.style_label_left, size=get_text_size(text_sz_opt))
            lbl_tp_txt    = label.new(end_x, tp_p, "🎯 ТЕЙК (TP): " + str.tostring(tp_p, "#.##"), color=color.new(c_bear, 20), textcolor=color.white, style=label.style_label_left, size=get_text_size(text_sz_opt))
            
            array.push(forecast_lines, l_entry)
            array.push(forecast_lines, l_sl)
            array.push(forecast_lines, l_tp)
            array.push(forecast_labels, lbl_entry_txt)
            array.push(forecast_labels, lbl_sl_txt)
            array.push(forecast_labels, lbl_tp_txt)

// Отрисовка сигналов MOEX на графике
plotshape(buy_signal, title="MOEX LONG Signal", style=shape.triangleup, location=location.belowbar, color=c_bull, size=size.normal, text="LONG MOEX", textcolor=color.black)
plotshape(sell_signal, title="MOEX SHORT Signal", style=shape.triangledown, location=location.abovebar, color=c_bear, size=size.normal, text="SHORT MOEX", textcolor=color.black)

// ==========================================================
// 5. ИНФО-ПАНЕЛЬ MOEX RUSSIA EDITION

var table moex_tbl = na
